## Narration 1101

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1111)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُجْمَعُ بَيْنَ الْمَرْأَةِ وَعَمَّتِهَا وَلاَ بَيْنَ الْمَرْأَةِ وَخَالَتِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1111]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said &quot;One cannot be married to a woman and her paternal aunt, or a woman and her maternal aunt at the same time</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1111]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के फूफी और भतीजी और खला और भांजी को (एक निकाह में) जमा न करे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1111]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ پھوپھی اور بھتیجی اور خالہ اور بھانجی کو جمع نہ کرے ۔</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ يُنْهَى أَنْ تُنْكَحَ الْمَرْأَةُ عَلَى عَمَّتِهَا أَوْ عَلَى خَالَتِهَا وَأَنْ يَطَأَ الرَّجُلُ وَلِيدَةً وَفِي بَطْنِهَا جَنِينٌ لِغَيْرِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1112" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;It is forbidden to be married to a woman and her paternal or maternal aunt at the same time, and for a man to have intercourse with a female slave who is carrying another man&#x27;s child</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1112]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1112" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سُئِلَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، تَزَوَّجَ امْرَأَةً ثُمَّ فَارَقَهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يُصِيبَهَا هَلْ تَحِلُّ لَهُ أُمُّهَا فَقَالَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ لاَ الأُمُّ مُبْهَمَةٌ لَيْسَ فِيهَا شَرْطٌ وَإِنَّمَا الشَّرْطُ فِي الرَّبَائِبِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Zayd ibn Thabit asked whether it was halal for a man who married a woman and then separated from her before he had cohabited with her, to marry her mother. Zayd ibn Thabit said, &quot;No. The mother is prohibited unconditionally. There are conditions, however about foster-mothers</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1113]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से, उन्होंने यह्या बिन सईद से रिवायत की के ज़ैद बिन साबित से पूछा गया के क्या उस शख़्स के लिए हलाल है जिसने किसी औरत से निकाह किया और हमबिस्तरी से पहले ही उस से जुदा हो गया, के वह उसकी माँ से निकाह कर ले। ज़ैद बिन साबित ने कहा: नहीं। माँ ग़ैर मशरूत तौर पर हराम है। अलबत्ता रज़ाई माओं के बारे में कुछ शरायत हैं।</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se, unhone Yahya bin Saeed se riwayat ki ke Zaid bin Thabit se pucha gaya ke kya us shakhs ke liye halal hai jisne kisi aurat se nikah kiya aur hambistari se pehle hi us se juda ho gaya, ke woh uski maa se nikah kar le. Zaid bin Thabit ne kaha: Nahi. Maa ghair mashroot tor par haram hai. Albata raza&#x27;i maaon ke bare mein kuch sharait hain.</p>

## Narration 1104

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1114)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ غَيْرِ، وَاحِدٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ مَسْعُودٍ، اسْتُفْتِيَ وَهُوَ بِالْكُوفَةِ عَنْ نِكَاحِ الأُمِّ، بَعْدَ الاِبْنَةِ إِذَا لَمْ تَكُنْ الاِبْنَةُ مُسَّتْ فَأَرْخَصَ فِي ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ إِنَّ ابْنَ مَسْعُودٍ قَدِمَ الْمَدِينَةَ فَسَأَلَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَأُخْبِرَ أَنَّهُ لَيْسَ كَمَا قَالَ وَإِنَّمَا الشَّرْطُ فِي الرَّبَائِبِ فَرَجَعَ ابْنُ مَسْعُودٍ إِلَى الْكُوفَةِ فَلَمْ يَصِلْ إِلَى مَنْزِلِهِ حَتَّى أَتَى الرَّجُلَ الَّذِي أَفْتَاهُ بِذَلِكَ فَأَمَرَهُ أَنْ يُفَارِقَ امْرَأَتَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ تَكُونُ تَحْتَهُ الْمَرْأَةُ ثُمَّ يَنْكِحُ أُمَّهَا فَيُصِيبُهَا إِنَّهَا تَحْرُمُ عَلَيْهِ امْرَأَتُهُ وَيُفَارِقُهُمَا جَمِيعًا وَيَحْرُمَانِ عَلَيْهِ أَبَدًا إِذَا كَانَ قَدْ أَصَابَ الأُمَّ فَإِنْ لَمْ يُصِبِ الأُمَّ لَمْ تَحْرُمْ عَلَيْهِ امْرَأَتُهُ وَفَارَقَ الأُمَّ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَتَزَوَّجُ الْمَرْأَةَ ثُمَّ يَنْكِحُ أُمَّهَا فَيُصِيبُهَا إِنَّهُ لاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ أُمُّهَا أَبَدًا وَلاَ تَحِلُّ لأَبِيهِ وَلاَ لاِبْنِهِ وَلاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ ابْنَتُهَا وَتَحْرُمُ عَلَيْهِ امْرَأَتُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَأَمَّا الزِّنَا فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يُحَرِّمُ شَيْئًا مِنْ ذَلِكَ لأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ ‏{‏وَأُمَّهَاتُ نِسَائِكُمْ‏}‏ فَإِنَّمَا حَرَّمَ مَا كَانَ تَزْوِيجًا وَلَمْ يَذْكُرْ تَحْرِيمَ الزِّنَا فَكُلُّ تَزْوِيجٍ كَانَ عَلَى وَجْهِ الْحَلاَلِ يُصِيبُ صَاحِبُهُ امْرَأَتَهُ فَهُوَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ التَّزْوِيجِ الْحَلاَلِ فَهَذَا الَّذِي سَمِعْتُ وَالَّذِي عَلَيْهِ أَمْرُ النَّاسِ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Masud (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from more than one source that when Abdullah ibn Masud was in Kufa, he was asked for an opinion about marrying the mother after marrying the daughter when the marriage with the daughter had not been consummated. He permitted it. When Ibn Masud came to Madina, he asked about it and was told that it was not as he had said, and that this condition referred to foster-mothers. Ibn Masud returnedto Kufa,and he had just reached his dwelling when the man who had asked him for the opinion came to visit and he ordered him to separate from his wife.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that if a man married the mother of a woman who was his wife and he had sexual relations with the mother then his wife was haram for him, and he had to separate from both of them. They were both haram to him forever, if he had had sexual relations with the mother. If he had not had relations with the mcther, his wife was not haram for him, and he separated from the mother. Malik explained further about the man who married a woman, and then married her mother and cohabited with her, &quot;The mother will never be halal for him, and she is not halal for his father or his son, and any daughters of hers are not halal for him and so his wife is haram for him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Fornication however, does not make any of that haram because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, mentioned &#x27;the mothers of your wives,&#x27; as one whom marriage made haram, and he didn&#x27;t mention the making haram by fornication. Every marriage in a halal manner in which a man cohabits with his wife, is a halal marriage. This is what I have heard, and this is how things are done among us</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से कई ज़रियों से रिवायत की कि जब अब्दुल्लाह बिन मसूद कूफ़ा में थे तोह उनसे बेटी के साथ निकाह के बाद माँ से निकाह के बारे में फतवा पूछा गया जबकि बेटी के साथ हमबिस्तरी न हुई थी. उन्होंने इजाज़त दे दी. जब इब्न मसूद मदीना आये तोह उनसे इस बारे में पूछा गया तोह उन्हें बताया गया कि मामला वैसा नहीं जैसा आप ने कहा था और यह शर्त राजा&#x27;ई माओं के बारे में थी. इब्न मसूद कूफ़ा लौटे और अभी घर ही पोहंचे थय के पूछने वाला शख्स आ गया तोह आप ने उसे बीवी से अलग होने का हुकुम दिया. मलिक ने कहा के अगर कोई शख्स अपनी बीवी की माँ से निकाह कर ले और माँ से सोहबत कर ले तोह उसकी बीवी उसके लिए हराम हो जाती है और उसे दोनों से अलग होना पड़ेगा. अगर सोहबत कर ली तोह दोनों हमेशा के लिए हराम हो गयीं. अगर सोहबत नहीं की तोह बीवी हराम नहीं होगी और वह सिर्फ माँ से अलग हो जायेगा. मलिक ने मज़ीद वज़ाहत की के अगर किसी ने औरत से निकाह किया फिर उसकी माँ से निकाह किया और सोहबत कर ली तोह माँ कभी उसके लिए हलाल नहीं होगी उसके लिए हलाल हैं क्युंके अल्लाह त&#x27;आला ने &#x27;तुम्हारी बीवियों की माएं&#x27; का ज़िक्र किया है जिन्हें निकाह हराम करता है हराम होने का ज़िक्र नहीं किया. हर वह निकाह जो हलाल तरीक़े से हो और आदमी बीवी से सोहबत करे मैंने यही सुना है और हमारे हाँ ऐसा ही किया जाता है.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے کئی ذرائع سے روایت کی کہ جب عبداللہ بن مسعود کوفہ میں تھے تو ان سے بیٹی کے ساتھ نکاح کے بعد ماں سے نکاح کے بارے میں فتویٰ پوچھا گیا جبکہ بیٹی کے ساتھ ہمبستری نہ ہوئی تھی۔ انہوں نے اجازت دے دی۔ جب ابن مسعود مدینہ آئے تو ان سے اس بارے میں پوچھا گیا تو انہیں بتایا گیا کہ معاملہ ویسا نہیں جیسا آپ نے کہا تھا اور یہ شرط رضاعی ماؤں کے بارے میں تھی۔ ابن مسعود کوفہ لوٹے اور ابھی گھر ہی پہنچے تھے کہ پوچھنے والا شخص آگیا تو آپ نے اسے بیوی سے الگ ہونے کا حکم دیا۔ مالک نے کہا کہ اگر کوئی شخص اپنی بیوی کی ماں سے نکاح کر لے اور ماں سے صحبت کر لے تو اس کی بیوی اس کے لیے حرام ہو جاتی ہے اور اسے دونوں سے الگ ہونا پڑے گا۔ اگر صحبت کر لی تو دونوں ہمیشہ کے لیے حرام ہو گئیں۔ اگر صحبت نہیں کی تو بیوی حرام نہیں ہوگی اور وہ صرف ماں سے الگ ہو جائے گا۔ مالک نے مزید وضاحت کی کہ اگر کسی نے عورت سے نکاح کیا پھر اس کی ماں سے نکاح کیا اور صحبت کر لی تو ماں کبھی اس کے لیے حلال نہیں ہوگی، نہ ہی وہ اس کے باپ یا بیٹے کے لیے حلال ہے، اور نہ ہی اس کی بیٹیاں اس کے لیے حلال ہیں، اس لیے اس کی بیوی بھی اس پر حرام ہے۔ مالک نے کہا کہ زنا ان میں سے کسی کو حرام نہیں کرتا کیونکہ اللہ تعالیٰ نے &#x27;تمہاری بیویوں کی مائیں&#x27; کا ذکر کیا ہے جنہیں نکاح حرام کرتا ہے، اور اس نے زنا سے حرام ہونے کا ذکر نہیں کیا۔ ہر وہ نکاح جو حلال طریقے سے ہو اور آدمی بیوی سے صحبت کرے، وہ حلال نکاح ہے۔ میں نے یہی سنا ہے اور ہمارے ہاں ایسا ہی کیا جاتا ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1114 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se kayi zarayon se riwayat ki ke jab Abdullah bin Masud Kufa mein the toh unse beti ke sath nikah ke baad maa se nikah ke bare mein fatwa pucha gaya jabke beti ke sath hambistari na hui thi. Unhone ijazat de di. Jab Ibn Masud Madina aaye toh unse is bare mein pucha gaya toh unhein bataya gaya ke mamla waisa nahi jaisa Aap ne kaha tha aur yeh shart raza&#x27;i maaon ke bare mein thi. Ibn Masud Kufa lautay aur abhi ghar hi pohanchay thay ke puchne wala shakhs aa gaya toh Aap ne use biwi se alag hone ka hukum diya. Malik ne kaha ke agar koi shakhs apni biwi ki maa se nikah kar le aur maa se sohbat kar le toh uski biwi uske liye haram ho jati hai aur use dono se alag hona parega. Agar sohbat kar li toh dono hamesha ke liye haram ho gayin. Agar sohbat nahi ki toh biwi haram nahi hogi aur woh sirf maa se alag ho jayega. Malik ne mazeed wazahat ki ke agar kisi ne aurat se nikah kiya phir uski maa se nikah kiya aur sohbat kar li toh maa kabhi uske liye halal nahi hogi, na hi woh uske baap ya bete ke liye halal hai, aur na hi uski betiyan uske liye halal hain, is liye uski biwi bhi us par haram hai. Malik ne kaha ke zina in mein se kisi ko haram nahi karta kyunke Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne &#x27;tumhari biwiyon ki maaein&#x27; ka zikr kiya hai jinhein nikah haram karta hai, aur usne zina se haram hone ka zikr nahi kiya. Har woh nikah jo halal tariqe se ho aur aadmi biwi se sohbat kare, woh halal nikah hai. Maine yahi suna hai aur hamare haan aisa hi kiya jata hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1105

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1115)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الشِّغَارِ وَالشِّغَارُ أَنْ يُزَوِّجَ الرَّجُلُ ابْنَتَهُ عَلَى أَنْ يُزَوِّجَهُ الآخَرُ ابْنَتَهُ لَيْسَ بَيْنَهُمَا صَدَاقٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1115</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1115 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said that a man who had committed fornication with a woman and the hadd-punishment had been applied to him for it, could marry that woman&#x27;s daughter and his son could marry the woman herself if he wished. That was because he had haram relations with her, and the relations Allah had made haram were from the relations made in a halal manner or in a manner resembling marriage. Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said, &quot;Do not marry the women your fathers have married. &quot; (Sura 4 ayat 21) Malik said, &quot;If a man were to marry a woman in her idda-period in a halal marriage and have relations with her, it would be haram for his son to marry the woman. That is because the father married her in a halal manner, and the hadd-punishment would not have been applied to him. Any child who was born to him would be attached to him as the father. Just as it would be haram for the son to marry a woman whom his father had married in her idda-period and had relations with, so the woman&#x27;s daughter would be haram for the father if he had had sexual relations with her.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade shighar, which meant one man giving his daughter in marriage to another man on the condition that the other gave his daughter to him in marriage without either of them paying the bride-price</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने शीघर से मन किया है. शीघ्र यह है के एक शख्स अपनी बेटी का निकाह एक शख्स से कर दे इस शर्त पर के दूसरा शख्स अपनी बेटी का निकाह उस से कर दे सिवाए इसके के कुछ मेहर न हो (हर एक की बेटी दूसरे की बेटी के लिए मेहर होगी).</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1115]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے شغار سے منع کیا ہے۔ شغار یہ ہے کہ ایک شخص اپنی بیٹی کا نکاح ایک شخص سے کر دے اس شرط پر کہ دوسرا شخص اپنی بیٹی کا نکاح اس سے کر دے سوائے اس کے کچھ مہر نہ ہو ( ہر ایک کی بیٹی دوسرے کی بیٹی کے لئے مہر ہوگی) ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne shighar se mana kiya hai. Shighar yeh hai ke ek shakhs apni beti ka nikah ek shakhs se kar de is shart par ke dusra shakhs apni beti ka nikah us se kar de siwaye iske ke kuch mehar na ho (har ek ki beti dusre ki beti ke liye mehar hogi).</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، وَمُجَمِّعٍ، ابْنَىْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ جَارِيَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ عَنْ خَنْسَاءَ بِنْتِ خِدَامٍ الأَنْصَارِيَّةِ، أَنَّ أَبَاهَا، زَوَّجَهَا وَهِيَ ثَيِّبٌ فَكَرِهَتْ ذَلِكَ فَأَتَتْ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَرَدَّ نِكَاحَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1116" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim from his father from Abd ar-Rahman and Mujamma the sons of Yazid ibn Jariya al-Ansari from Khansa bint Khidam al-Ansariya that her father gave her in marriage and she had been previously married. She disapproved of that, and went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he revoked the marriage</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1116]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1116" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ الْمَكِّيِّ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، أُتِيَ بِنِكَاحٍ لَمْ يَشْهَدْ عَلَيْهِ إِلاَّ رَجُلٌ وَامْرَأَةٌ فَقَالَ هَذَا نِكَاحُ السِّرِّ وَلاَ أُجِيزُهُ وَلَوْ كُنْتُ تَقَدَّمْتُ فِيهِ لَرَجَمْتُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zubayr al-Makki that a case was brought to Umar about a marriage which had only been witnessed by one man and one woman . He said, &quot;This is a secret marriage and I do not permit it. Had I been the first to come upon it, I would have ordered them to be stoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1117]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1117" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू जुबैर से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्र खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के सामने एक निकाह का ज़िक्र आया जिसका कोई गवाह न था सिवाए एक मर्द और एक औरत के. आप ने फ़रमाया यह चोरी छुपे का निकाह है पहले इसको बयां कर चूका होता तो अब मैं राजम करता.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَعَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ طُلَيْحَةَ الأَسَدِيَّةَ، كَانَتْ تَحْتَ رُشَيْدٍ الثَّقَفِيِّ فَطَلَّقَهَا فَنَكَحَتْ فِي عِدَّتِهَا فَضَرَبَهَا عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَضَرَبَ زَوْجَهَا بِالْمِخْفَقَةِ ضَرَبَاتٍ وَفَرَّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا ثُمَّ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَيُّمَا امْرَأَةٍ نَكَحَتْ فِي عِدَّتِهَا فَإِنْ كَانَ زَوْجُهَا الَّذِي تَزَوَّجَهَا لَمْ يَدْخُلْ بِهَا فُرِّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا ثُمَّ اعْتَدَّتْ بَقِيَّةَ عِدَّتِهَا مِنْ زَوْجِهَا الأَوَّلِ ثُمَّ كَانَ الآخَرُ خَاطِبًا مِنَ الْخُطَّابِ وَإِنْ كَانَ دَخَلَ بِهَا فُرِّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا ثُمَّ اعْتَدَّتْ بَقِيَّةَ عِدَّتِهَا مِنَ الأَوَّلِ ثُمَّ اعْتَدَّتْ مِنَ الآخَرِ ثُمَّ لاَ يَجْتَمِعَانِ أَبَدًا ‏.‏ قَالَ</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab and from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Tulayha al-Asadiya was the wife of Rushayd ath-Thaqafi. He divorced her, and she got married in her idda-period. Umar ibn al-Khattab beat her and her husband with a stick several times, and separated them. Then Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;If a woman marries in her idda-period, and the new husband has not consummated the marriage, then separate them, and when she has completed the idda of her first husband, the other becomes a suitor. If he has consummated the marriage then separate them. Then she must complete her idda from her first husband, and then the idda from the other one, and they are never to be reunited.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik added, &#x27;&#x27;Said ibn al-Musayyab said that she had her dowry because he had consummated the marriage.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1118]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said,&quot;The practice with us concerning a free woman whose husband dies, is that she does an idda of four months and ten days and she does not marry if she doubts her period until she is free of any doubt or if she fears that she is pregnant</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसैय्यब और सुलैमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के तलहा अल-असदिया, राशिद सक़फ़ी के निकाह में थीं। उन्होंने तलाक़ दी तो तलहा अल-असदिया ने इद्दत के अंदर दूसरे शख़्स से निकाह कर लिया। हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने दोनों को कोड़े मारे और निकाह छुड़वा दिया। फिर फ़रमाया के औरत इद्दत में निकाह करे किसी और शख़्स से, तो अगर जमा न किया हो तो निकाह छोड़ कर पहले ख़ावंद की जिस क़दर इद्दत बाक़ी हो पूरी करे, अब जिस से जी चाहे निकाह करे। दूसरे ख़ावंद से ज़िंदगी भर निकाह नहीं हो सकता। सईद बिन मुसैय्यब ने कहा के वोह औरत दूसरे ख़ावंद से अपना मेहर ले सकती है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ طلحہ الاسدیہ رشید ثقفی کے نکاح میں تھیں انہوں نے طلاق دی تو طلحہ الاسدیہ نے عدت کے اندر دوسرے شخص سے نکاح کر لیا حضرت عمر نے دونوں کو کوڑے مارے اور نکاح چھڑوا دیا پھر فرمایا کہ عورت عدت میں نکاح کرے کسی اور شخص سے تو اگر جماع نہ کیا ہو تو نکاح چھوڑ کر پہلے خاوند کی جس قدر عدت باقی ہو پوری کرے اب جس سے جی چاہے نکاح کرے دوسرے خاوند سے زندگی بھر نکاح نہیں ہو سکتا سعید بن مسیب نے کہا کہ وہ عورت دوسرے خاوند سے اپنا مہر لے سکتی ہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Talha al-Asadiya, Rashid Saqafi ke nikah mein thin. Unhone talaq di to Talha al-Asadiya ne iddat ke andar dusre shakhs se nikah kar liya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne dono ko kode mare aur nikah chhudwa diya. Phir farmaya ke aurat iddat mein nikah kare kisi aur shakhs se, to agar jama na kiya ho to nikah chhod kar pehle khawand ki jis qadar iddat baqi ho puri kare, ab jis se ji chahe nikah kare. Dusre khawand se zindagi bhar nikah nahi ho sakta. Saeed bin Musayyib ne kaha ke woh aurat dusre khawand se apna mehar le sakti hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1118" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، وَعَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرُ، سُئِلاَ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، كَانَتْ تَحْتَهُ امْرَأَةٌ حُرَّةٌ فَأَرَادَ أَنْ يَنْكِحَ عَلَيْهَا أَمَةً فَكَرِهَا أَنْ يَجْمَعَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1119" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion); Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Abdullah ibn Abbas and Abdullah ibn Umar were asked about a man who had a free woman as a wife and then wanted to marry a slave-girl. They disapproved that he should combine the two of them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1119]</p>

## Narration 1110

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1120)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1120</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1120 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ لاَ تُنْكَحُ الأَمَةُ عَلَى الْحُرَّةِ إِلاَّ أَنْ تَشَاءَ الْحُرَّةُ فَإِنْ طَاعَتِ الْحُرَّةُ فَلَهَا الثُّلُثَانِ مِنَ الْقَسْمِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ يَنْبَغِي لِحُرٍّ أَنْ يَتَزَوَّجَ أَمَةً وَهُوَ يَجِدُ طَوْلاً لِحُرَّةٍ وَلاَ يَتَزَوَّجَ أَمَةً إِذَا لَمْ يَجِدْ طَوْلاً لِحُرَّةٍ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَخْشَى الْعَنَتَ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَمَنْ لَمْ يَسْتَطِعْ مِنْكُمْ طَوْلاً أَنْ يَنْكِحَ الْمُحْصَنَاتِ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ فَمِمَّا مَلَكَتْ أَيْمَانُكُمْ مِنْ فَتَيَاتِكُمُ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ‏}‏ وَقَالَ ‏{‏ذَلِكَ لِمَنْ خَشِيَ الْعَنَتَ مِنْكُمْ‏}‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْعَنَتُ هُوَ الزِّنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;The slave girl is not married when there is a free woman who is a wife unless the free woman wishes it. If the free woman complies, she has two-thirds of the division of time.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1120</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1120 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;A free man must not marry a slave-girl when he can afford to marry a free-woman, and he should not marry a slave-girl when he cannot afford a free woman unless he fears fornication. That is because Allah, may he be Blessed and Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;If you are not affluent enough to marry believing women, who are muhsanat, take slave-girls who are believing women that your right hands own.&#x27; (Sura 4 ayat 24) He says, &#x27;That is for those of you who fear al-anat.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;Al-anat is fornication</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब कहते थे के आज़ाद औरत के होते हुए लौंड़ी से निकाह न किया जायेगा मगर जब आज़ाद औरत राज़ी हो जाये (तो) दो दिन खावंद उसके पास रहेगा और एक दिन लौंड़ी के पास. कहा मालिक ने आज़ाद औरत से निकाह करने की क़ुदरत हो तो लौंडी से निकाह न करे और अगर आज़ाद औरत से निकाह करने की क़ुदरत न हो तो भी लौंड़ी से निकाह न करे मगर इस हाल में के ज़िना का खौफ हो औरतों से निकाह करने की तो मुस्लमान लौंडियों से निकाह कर ले और यह उस शख्स के वास्ते है जो तुम में से जीने का ख़ौफ़ करे.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب کہتے تھے کہ آزاد عورت کے ہوتے ہوئے لونڈی سے نکاح نہ کیا جائے گا مگر جب آزاد عورت راضی ہو جائے دو دن خاوند اس کے پاس رہے گا اور ایک دن لونڈی کے پاس ۔ کہا مالک نے آزاد عورت سے نکاح کرنے کی قدرت ہو تو لونڈی سے نکاح نہ کرے اور اگر آزاد عورت سے نکاح کرنے کی قدرت نہ ہو تو بھی لونڈی سے نکاح نہ کرے مگر اس حال میں کہ زنا کا خوف ہو کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ نے فرمایا کہ جو شخص تم میں سے قدرت نہ رکھے آزاد مسلمان عورتوں سے نکاح کرنے کی تو مسلمان لونڈیوں سے نکاح کر لے اور یہ اس شخص کے واسطے ہے جو تم میں سے زنا کا خوف کرے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib kehte thay ke azad aurat ke hote hue laundi se nikah na kiya jayega magar jab azad aurat razi ho jaye (to) do din khawand uske paas rahega aur ek din laundi ke paas. Kaha Malik ne azad aurat se nikah karne ki qudrat ho to laundi se nikah na kare aur agar azad aurat se nikah karne ki qudrat na ho to bhi laundi se nikah na kare magar is haal mein ke zina ka khauf ho, kyunke Allah Jalla Jalalahu ne farmaya ke jo shakhs tum mein se qudrat na rakhe azad musalman aurton se nikah karne ki to musalman laundiyon se nikah kar le aur yeh us shakhs ke waste hai jo tum mein se zina ka khauf kare.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1111

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1121)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي الرَّجُلِ يُطَلِّقُ الأَمَةَ ثَلاَثًا ثُمَّ يَشْتَرِيهَا إِنَّهَا لاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ حَتَّى تَنْكِحَ زَوْجًا غَيْرَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Abu Abd ar- Rahman that Zayd ibn Thabit said that if a man divorced his slave-girl three times and then bought her, she was not halal for him until she had married another husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1121]</p>

## Narration 1112

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1122)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1122]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1122</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1122 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، سُئِلاَ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، زَوَّجَ عَبْدًا لَهُ جَارِيَةً فَطَلَّقَهَا الْعَبْدُ الْبَتَّةَ ثُمَّ وَهَبَهَا سَيِّدُهَا لَهُ هَلْ تَحِلُّ لَهُ بِمِلْكِ الْيَمِينِ فَقَالاَ لاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ حَتَّى تَنْكِحَ زَوْجًا غَيْرَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab and Sulayman ibn Yasar were asked whether, when a man married a slave of his to a slave-girl and the slave divorced her irrevocably, and then her master gave her to the slave, she was then halal for the slave by the possession of the right hand. They said, &quot;No. She is not halal until she has married another husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1122]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1122 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन साबित (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) फ़रमाते थे के जो शख़्स लौंडी को तीन तलाक़ें दे कर ख़रीद ले तो सोहबत करना दुरुस्त नहीं जब तक दूसरा निकाह न कर ले। सईद बिन मुसैय्यब और सुलैमान बिन यसार से सवाल हुआ के एक शख़्स ने अपने ग़ुलाम का अपनी लौंडी से निकाह कर दिया, ग़ुलाम ने लौंडी को दो तलाक़ें दे दीं। इसके बाद मौला ने वोह लौंडी ग़ुलाम को हिबा कर दी, अब वोह लौंडी ग़ुलाम को दुरुस्त है या नहीं? इन दोनों ने जवाब दिया दुरुस्त नहीं यहां तक के किसी और से निकाह न कर ले।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن ثابت کہتے تھے جو شخص لونڈی کو تین طلاقیں دے کر خرید لے تو صحبت کرنا درست نہیں جب تک دوسرا نکاح نہ کرلے ۔ سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے سوال ہوا کہ ایک شخص نے اپنے غلام کا اپنی لونڈی سے نکاح کر دیا غلام نے لونڈی کو دو طلاقیں دیں اس کے بعد مولیٰ نے وہ لونڈی غلام کو ہبہ کر دی اب وہ لونڈی غلام کو درست ہے یا نہیں ان دونوں نے جواب دیا درست نہیں یہاں تک کہ کسی اور سے نکاح نہ کرلے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Thabit (Radi Allahu Anhu) farmate thay ke jo shakhs laundi ko teen talaqen de kar khareed le to sohbat karna durust nahi jab tak dusra nikah na kar le. Saeed bin Musayyab aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se sawal hua ke ek shakhs ne apne ghulam ka apni laundi se nikah kar diya, ghulam ne laundi ko do talaqen de di. Iske baad maula ne woh laundi ghulam ko hiba kar di, ab woh laundi ghulam ko durust hai ya nahi? In dono ne jawab diya durust nahi yahan tak ke kisi aur se nikah na kar le.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، كَانَتْ تَحْتَهُ أَمَةٌ مَمْلُوكَةٌ فَاشْتَرَاهَا وَقَدْ كَانَ طَلَّقَهَا وَاحِدَةً فَقَالَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ بِمِلْكِ يَمِينِهِ مَا لَمْ يَبُتَّ طَلاَقَهَا فَإِنْ بَتَّ طَلاَقَهَا فَلاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ بِمِلْكِ يَمِينِهِ حَتَّى تَنْكِحَ زَوْجًا غَيْرَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَنْكِحُ الأَمَةَ فَتَلِدُ مِنْهُ ثُمَّ يَبْتَاعُهَا إِنَّهَا لاَ تَكُونُ أُمَّ وَلَدٍ لَهُ بِذَلِكَ الْوَلَدِ الَّذِي وَلَدَتْ مِنْهُ وَهِيَ لِغَيْرِهِ حَتَّى تَلِدَ مِنْهُ وَهِيَ فِي مِلْكِهِ بَعْدَ ابْتِيَاعِهِ إِيَّاهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنِ اشْتَرَاهَا وَهِيَ حَامِلٌ مِنْهُ ثُمَّ وَضَعَتْ عِنْدَهُ كَانَتْ أُمَّ وَلَدِهِ بِذَلِكَ الْحَمْلِ فِيمَا نُرَى وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had asked Ibn Shihab about a man who had a slave-girl as a wife, and then he bought her, and divorced her once. He said, &quot;She is halal for him by the possession of the right hand as long as he does not make his divorce irrevocable. If he irrevocably divorces her, she is not halal for him by the possession of the right hand until she has married another husband.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1123]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1123</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1123 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that if a man rnarried a female slave and then she had a child by him, and then he bought her, she was not an umm walad for him because of the child born to him while she belonged to another, until she had had a child by him while she was in his possession after he had purchased her. Malik said, &quot;If he buys her and she is pregnant by him and she then gives birth while she belongs to him, she is his umm walad by that pregnancy, according to what we think, and Allah knows best</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक (रहिमहुल्लाह) ने कहा: एक शख़्स निकाह करे एक लौंडी से, फिर उस से बच्चा पैदा हो, उसके बाद लौंडी को ख़रीद ले, तो वोह लौंडी पहले बच्चे की वजह से उसकी उम्म-ए-वलद न होगी। अलबत्ता अगर ख़रीदने के बाद दूसरा बच्चा मालिक से पैदा हुआ तो उम्म-ए-वलद हो जाएगी। और जिस ने उस लौंडी को ख़रीदा हामिल (प्रेग्नेंट) की हालत में, वोह हमल ख़रीदने वाले का था, उसके पास आ कर जने (बच्चा जने) तो उम्म-ए-वलद हो जाएगी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik (Rahimahullah) ne kaha: Ek shakhs nikah kare ek laundi se, phir us se bacha paida ho, uske baad laundi ko khareed le, to woh laundi pehle bache ki wajah se uski umm-e-walad na hogi. Albatta agar khareedne ke baad dusra bacha malik se paida hua to umm-e-walad ho jayegi. Aur jis ne us laundi ko khareeda hamil (pregnant) ki halat mein, woh hamal khareedne wale ka tha, uske paas aa kar jane (bacha jame) to umm-e-walad ho jayegi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، سُئِلَ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ، وَابْنَتِهَا، مِنْ مِلْكِ الْيَمِينِ تُوطَأُ إِحْدَاهُمَا بَعْدَ الأُخْرَى فَقَالَ عُمَرُ مَا أُحِبُّ أَنْ أَخْبُرَهُمَا جَمِيعًا ‏.‏ وَنَهَى عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1124" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al- Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud from his father that Umar ibn al- Khattab was asked about a woman and her daughter who were in the possession of the right hand, and whether one could have intercourse with one of them after the other Umar said, &quot;I dislike both being permitted together.&quot; He then forbade that</p>

## Narration 1115

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1125)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1125</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1125 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ قَبِيصَةَ بْنِ ذُؤَيْبٍ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، سَأَلَ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ عَنِ الأُخْتَيْنِ، مِنْ مِلْكِ الْيَمِينِ هَلْ يُجْمَعُ بَيْنَهُمَا فَقَالَ عُثْمَانُ أَحَلَّتْهُمَا آيَةٌ وَحَرَّمَتْهُمَا آيَةٌ فَأَمَّا أَنَا فَلاَ أُحِبُّ أَنْ أَصْنَعَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَخَرَجَ مِنْ عِنْدِهِ فَلَقِيَ رَجُلاً مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ لَوْ كَانَ لِي مِنَ الأَمْرِ شَىْءٌ ثُمَّ وَجَدْتُ أَحَدًا فَعَلَ ذَلِكَ لَجَعَلْتُهُ نَكَالاً ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ أُرَاهُ عَلِيَّ بْنَ أَبِي طَالِبٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Uthman ibn Affan (companion). The two separately attributed responses remain distinct. The second Companion is unnamed in the report; Ibn Shihab’s conjecture identifying him is retained as attribution context, not converted to certainty. Report type: companion report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The two separately attributed responses remain distinct. The second Companion is unnamed in the report; Ibn Shihab’s conjecture identifying him is retained as attribution context, not converted to certainty.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Qabisa ibn Dhu&#x27;ayb that a man asked Uthman ibn Affan whether one could have intercourse with two sisters who one owned. Uthman said, &quot;One ayat makes them halal, and one ayat makes them haram. As for me, I wouldn&#x27;t like to do it.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">The man left him and met one of the companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and asked him about it, and he said, &quot;Had I any authority and I found someone who had done it, I would punish him as an example.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1125 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ाबिसा बिन जुऐब से रिवायत है के एक शख्स ने उस्मान बिन अफ्फान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पुछा के दो बहनों को मुल्क-इ-यामीन से रखना दुरुस्त है या नहीं? हज़रात उस्मान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया के एक आयत की रू से दुरुस्त है और दूसरी आयत की रू से दुरुस्त नहीं है मगर मैं इसको पसंद नहीं करता. फिर वह शख्स चला गया और एक और सहबी से मिला देता. इब्न शिहाब ने कहा मैं समझता हूँ वह सहाबी हज़रत अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) थे. जुबैर बिन एव्वं (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से भी ऐसी ही रिवायत है. मालिक (रहिमहुल्लाह) ने कहा अगर किसी शख़्स के पास एक लौंडी हो और वह उस से जमा&#x27; करे फिर उसकी बहन से जमा&#x27; करना चाहे तो यह दुरुस्त नहीं है जब तक पेहली बेहेन की फ़र्ज अपने ऊपर हराम न करे मसलन उसका निकाह कर दे या अपने घुलम से ब्याह कर दे.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قبیصہ بن ذویب سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے عثمان بن عفان سے پوچھا کہ دو بہنوں کو ملک یمین سے رکھنا درست ہے یا نہیں حضرت عثماں نے فرمایا کہ ایک آیت کی رو سے درست ہے اور دوسری آیت کی رو سے درست نہیں ہے مگر میں اس کو پسند نہیں کرتا پھر وہ شخص چلا گیا اور ایک اور صحابی سے ملا ان سے بھی یہی مسئلہ پوچھا انہوں نے کہا اگر میں حاکم ہوتا اور کسی کو ایسا کرتے دیکھتا تو سخت سزا دیتا ابن شہاب نے کہا میں سمجھتا ہوں وہ صحابی حضرت علی تھے ۔ زبیر بن عوام سے بھی ایسی ہی روایت ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر کسی شخص کے پاس ایک لونڈی ہو اور وہ اس سے جماع کرے پھر اس کی بہن سے جماع کرنا چاہے تو یہ درست نہیں ہے جب تک پہلی بہن کی فرج اپنے اوپر حرام نہ کرے مثلا اس کا نکاح کر دے یا اپنے غلام سے بیاہ کر دے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1125</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1125 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qabisa bin Zuaib se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Usman bin Affan (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha ke do behno ko mulk-e-yameen se rakhna durust hai ya nahi? Hazrat Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya ke ek ayat ki roo se durust hai aur dusri ayat ki roo se durust nahi hai magar main isko pasand nahi karta. Phir woh shakhs chala gaya aur ek aur sahabi se mila, un se bhi yahi masala poocha. Unhone kaha agar main hakim hota aur kisi ko aisa karte dekhta to sakht saza deta. Ibn Shihab ne kaha main samajhta hoon woh sahabi Hazrat Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) thay. Zubair bin Awwam (Radi Allahu Anhu) se bhi aisi hi riwayat hai. Malik (Rahimahullah) ne kaha agar kisi shakhs ke paas ek laundi ho aur woh us se jama&#x27; kare phir uski behen se jama&#x27; karna chahe to yeh durust nahi hai jab tak pehli behen ki farj apne upar haram na kare, maslan uska nikah kar de ya apne ghulam se byah kar de.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1116

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1126)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1126]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1126</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1126 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنِ الزُّبَيْرِ بْنِ الْعَوَّامِ، مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الأَمَةِ تَكُونُ عِنْدَ الرَّجُلِ فَيُصِيبُهَا ثُمَّ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يُصِيبَ أُخْتَهَا إِنَّهَا لاَ تَحِلُّ لَهُ حَتَّى يُحَرِّمَ عَلَيْهِ فَرْجَ أُخْتِهَا بِنِكَاحٍ أَوْ عِتَاقَةٍ أَوْ كِتَابَةٍ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ يُزَوِّجُهَا عَبْدَهُ أَوْ غَيْرَ عَبْدِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: az-Zubayr ibn al-Awwam (companion). The exact source attributes only “the like of that” to az-Zubayr. No expanded quotation is borrowed from another entry.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that az-Zubayr ibn al-Awwam said the like of that.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1126]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1126</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1126 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that if a man had sexual relations with a female slave that he owned, and then he wanted to also have relations with her sister, the sister was not halal for a man until intercourse with the slave-girl had been made haram for him by marriage, setting free, kitaba, or the like of that - for instance, if he had married her to his slave or someone other than his slave</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्होंने सुना के ज़ुबैर बिन अल-अव्वम ने भी ऐसा ही कहा था। मालिक ने कहा के अगर किसी आदमी ने अपनी लौंडी से सोहबत की और फिर वोह उसकी बहन से भी सोहबत करना चाहे तोह उसके लिए उसकी बहन उस वक़्त तक हलाल नहीं जब तक के उस लौंडी से हमबिस्तरी उसके लिए निकाह, आज़ादी, किताबत या उस जैसी किसी और चीज़ से हराम न हो जाए, मिसाल के तौर पर अगर उसने उसे अपने ग़ुलाम या किसी और से ब्याह दिया हो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ زبیر بن العوام نے بھی ایسا ہی کہا تھا۔ مالک نے کہا کہ اگر کسی آدمی نے اپنی لونڈی سے صحبت کی اور پھر وہ اس کی بہن سے بھی صحبت کرنا چاہے تو اس کے لیے اس کی بہن اس وقت تک حلال نہیں جب تک کہ اس لونڈی سے ہمبستری اس کے لیے نکاح، آزادی، کتابت یا اس جیسی کسی اور چیز سے حرام نہ ہو جائے، مثال کے طور پر اگر اس نے اسے اپنے غلام یا کسی اور سے بیاہ دیا ہو۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhone suna ke Zubair bin al-Awwam ne bhi aisa hi kaha tha. Malik ne kaha ke agar kisi aadmi ne apni londi se sohbat ki aur phir woh uski behen se bhi sohbat karna chahe toh uske liye uski behen us waqt tak halal nahi jab tak ke us londi se hambistari uske liye nikah, azadi, kitabat ya us jaisi kisi aur cheez se haram na ho jaye, misaal ke tor par agar usne use apne ghulam ya kisi aur se byah diya ho.</p>

## Narration 1117

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، وَهَبَ لاِبْنِهِ جَارِيَةً فَقَالَ لاَ تَمَسَّهَا فَإِنِّي قَدْ كَشَفْتُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn al-Khattab gave his son a slave-girl and said, &quot;Do not touch her, for I have uncovered her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1127]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Salim ibn Abdullah (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abd arRahman ibn al-Mujabbir said that Salim ibn Abdullah gave his son a slave-girl and said, &quot;Do not go near her, for I wanted her, and did not act towards her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1128]</p>

## Narration 1119

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1129)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1129]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1129" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1129</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1129 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ أَبَا نَهْشَلِ بْنَ الأَسْوَدِ، قَالَ لِلْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ إِنِّي رَأَيْتُ جَارِيَةً لِي مُنْكَشِفًا عَنْهَا وَهِيَ فِي الْقَمَرِ فَجَلَسْتُ مِنْهَا مَجْلِسَ الرَّجُلِ مِنِ امْرَأَتِهِ فَقَالَتْ إِنِّي حَائِضٌ فَقُمْتُ فَلَمْ أَقْرَبْهَا بَعْدُ أَفَأَهَبُهَا لاِبْنِي يَطَؤُهَا فَنَهَاهُ الْقَاسِمُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1129" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Abu Nahshal ibn al-Aswad said to al-Qasim ibn Muhammad,&quot;I saw a slave-girl of mine uncovered in the moonlight, and so I sat on her as a man sits on a woman. She said that she was menstruating, so I stood up and have not gone near her after that. Can I give her to my son to have intercourse with?&quot; Al-Qasim forbade that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1129]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया बिन सईद से रिवायत है के अबू नहशल बिन असवद ने कासिम बिन मुहम्मद से कहा के मैं ने अपनी लौंडी को नंगा देखा चाँदनी में, तो मैं उसके पैर उठा कर जमा&#x27; को मुस्तइद हो गया, वह बोली हैज़ा हूँ तो मैं उठ खड़ा हुआ. अब मैं इस लौंडी को हिबा कर दूँ अपने बेटे को ताके वह इस से जमा&#x27; करे. कासिम बिन मुहम्मद ने मना किया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1129" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Saeed se riwayat hai ke Abu Nahshal bin Aswad ne Qasim bin Muhammad se kaha ke main ne apni laundi ko nanga dekha chandni mein, to main uske pair utha kar jama&#x27; ko mustaid ho gaya, woh boli haiza hoon to main uth khada hua. <details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْلَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ مَرْوَانَ، أَنَّهُ وَهَبَ لِصَاحِبٍ لَهُ جَارِيَةً ثُمَّ سَأَلَهُ عَنْهَا فَقَالَ قَدْ هَمَمْتُ أَنْ أَهَبَهَا لاِبْنِي فَيَفْعَلَ بِهَا كَذَا وَكَذَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ الْمَلِكِ لَمَرْوَانُ كَانَ أَوْرَعَ مِنْكَ وَهَبَ لاِبْنِهِ جَارِيَةً ثُمَّ قَالَ لاَ تَقْرَبْهَا فَإِنِّي قَدْ رَأَيْتُ سَاقَهَا مُنْكَشِفَةً ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1130" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan (unresolved); Marwan (unresolved). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibrahim ibn Abi Abla from Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan that he gave a slave-girl to a friend of his, and later asked him about her. He said, &quot;I intended to give her to my son to do such-and-such with her.&quot; Abd al-Malik said, &quot;Marwan was more scrupulous than you. He gave a slave-girl to his son, and then he said, &#x27;Do not go near her, for I have seen her leg uncovered</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1130]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1130" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1130</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1130 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल मलिक बिन मरवान ने अपने दोस्त को एक लौंडी हीबा की फिर उस से उस लौंड़ी का हाल पुछा के मैं इस लौंड़ी को हिबा कर दूं अपने बेटे को ताकि वह इस से जमा&#x27; करे. अब्दुल मलिक ने कहा के मरवान तुझ से ज़्यादा परहेज़गार था खुली हुई देखि थीं. मालिक (रहिमहुल्लाह) ने कहा: यहूदी लौंड़ी और नसरानी लौंड़ी से निकाह करना दुरूस्त नहीं और अल्लाह जल्ला जलालहु ने अपनी किताब में जो अहल-इ-किताब की औरतों से निकाह दुरुस्त किया है हैं. और अल्लाह जल्ला जलालहु ने फ़रमाया जो शख्स तुम में से मुस्लमान आज़ाद औरतों से निकाह करने की ताक़त न रखे तो वह मुसलमान लौंडियों से निकाह करे. अल्लाह ने मुस्लमान लौंडियों से निकाह करना हलाल किया है न के अहल-इ-किताब की लौंडियों से. अलबत्ता यहूदी या नसरानी लौंड़ी से उसके मालिक को जमा&#x27; करना दुरूस्त है मगर मुश्रिका लौंड़ी से दुरुस्त नहीं.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1130" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالملک بن مروان نے اپنے دوست کو ایک لونڈی ہبہ کی پھر اس سے اس لونڈی کا حال پوچھا اس نے کہا میرا ارادہ ہے کہ میں اس لونڈی کو ہبہ کر دوں اپنے بیٹے کو تاکہ وہ اس سے جماع کرے عبدالملک نے کہا کہ مروان تجھ سے زیادہ پرہیز گار تھا اس نے اپنے بیٹے کو ایک لونڈی ہبہ کی اور کہہ دیا اس سے صحبت نہ کرنا کیونکہ میں نے اس کی پنڈلیاں کھلی ہوئی دیکھی تھیں ۔ کہا مالک نے یہودی لونڈی اور نصرانی لونڈی سے نکاح کرنا درست نہیں اور اللہ جل جلالہ نے اپنی کتاب میں جو اہل کتاب کی عورتوں سے نکاح درست کیا ہے اس سے آزاد عورتیں مراد ہیں اور اللہ جل جلالہ نے فرمایا جو شخص تم میں سے مسلمان آزاد عورتوں سے نکاح کرنے کی طاقت نہ رکھے تو وہ مسلمان لونڈیوں سے نکاح کرے اللہ نے مسلمان لونڈیوں سے نکاح کرنا حلال کیا ہے نہ کہ اہل کتاب کی لونڈیوں سے البتہ یہودی یا نصرانی لونڈی سے اس کے مالک کو جماع کرنا درست ہے مگر مشرکہ لونڈی سے درست نہیں ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1130" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1130 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdul Malik bin Marwan ne apne dost ko ek laundi hiba ki phir us se us laundi ka haal poocha, usne kaha mera irada hai ke main is laundi ko hiba kar doon apne bete ko taake woh is se jama&#x27; kare. Abdul Malik ne kaha ke Marwan tujh se zyada parhezgar tha, usne apne bete ko ek laundi hiba ki aur keh diya is se sohbat na karna kyunke main ne iski pindliyan khuli hui dekhi thin. Malik (Rahimahullah) ne kaha: Yahudi laundi aur Nasrani laundi se nikah karna durust nahi aur Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ne apni kitab mein jo Ahl-e-Kitab ki aurton se nikah durust kiya hai, us se azad aurtein murad hain. Aur Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ne farmaya jo shakhs tum mein se musalman azad aurton se nikah karne ki taqat na rakhe to woh musalman laundiyon se nikah kare. Allah ne musalman laundiyon se nikah karna halal kiya hai na ke Ahl-e-Kitab ki laundiyon se. Albatta Yahudi ya Nasrani laundi se uske malik ko jama&#x27; karna durust hai magar mushrika laundi se durust nahi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1130" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1121

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1131)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ الْمُحْصَنَاتُ مِنَ النِّسَاءِ هُنَّ أُولاَتُ الأَزْوَاجِ وَيَرْجِعُ ذَلِكَ إِلَى أَنَّ اللَّهَ حَرَّمَ الزِّنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1131" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab interpreting muhsanat in the explicitly supplied chain. The long opening is Maliks legal commentary. A distinct transmitted saying of Said follows and supplies his actual definition. Select that attributed saying only; the final explanatory conclusion remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Said ibn al- Musayyab said, &quot;The muhsanat among women are those who have husbands.&quot; </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1131]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1131" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1131</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1131 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;It is not halal to marry a christian or jewish slave-girl because Allah the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;Believing women who are muhsanat and women of those who were given the Book before you who are muhsanat&#x27;, (sura 5 ayat 6) and they are free women from the Christians and Jews. Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;If you are not affluent enough to marry believing women who are muhsanat, take believing slave-girls whom your right hands own.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 4 ayat 24) Malik said, &quot;In our opinion, Allah made marriage to believing slave-girls halal, and He did not make halal marriage to christian and jewish slave-girls from the People of the Book.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The christian and jewish slave-girl are halal for their master by right of possession, but intercourse with a magian slave-girl is not halal by the right of possession.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1131" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">That referred to the fact that Allah has made fornication haram</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1131" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1122

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1132)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1132]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1132" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1132</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1132 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، وَبَلَغَهُ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَنَّهُمَا كَانَا يَقُولاَنِ إِذَا نَكَحَ الْحُرُّ الأَمَةَ فَمَسَّهَا فَقَدْ أَحْصَنَتْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكُلُّ مَنْ أَدْرَكْتُ كَانَ يَقُولُ ذَلِكَ تُحْصِنُ الأَمَةُ الْحُرَّ إِذَا نَكَحَهَا فَمَسَّهَا فَقَدْ أَحْصَنَتْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ يُحْصِنُ الْعَبْدُ الْحُرَّةَ إِذَا مَسَّهَا بِنِكَاحٍ وَلاَ تُحْصِنُ الْحُرَّةُ الْعَبْدَ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَعْتِقَ وَهُوَ زَوْجُهَا فَيَمَسَّهَا بَعْدَ عِتْقِهِ فَإِنْ فَارَقَهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَعْتِقَ فَلَيْسَ بِمُحْصَنٍ حَتَّى يَتَزَوَّجَ بَعْدَ عِتْقِهِ وَيَمَسَّ امْرَأَتَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمَةُ إِذَا كَانَتْ تَحْتَ الْحُرِّ ثُمَّ فَارَقَهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ تَعْتِقَ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يُحْصِنُهَا نِكَاحُهُ إِيَّاهَا وَهِيَ أَمَةٌ حَتَّى تُنْكَحَ بَعْدَ عِتْقِهَا وَيُصِيبَهَا زَوْجُهَا فَذَلِكَ إِحْصَانُهَا وَالأَمَةُ إِذَا كَانَتْ تَحْتَ الْحُرِّ فَتَعْتِقُ وَهِيَ تَحْتَهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُفَارِقَهَا فَإِنَّهُ يُحْصِنُهَا إِذَا عَتَقَتْ وَهِيَ عِنْدَهُ إِذَا هُوَ أَصَابَهَا بَعْدَ أَنْ تَعْتِقَ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْحُرَّةُ النَّصْرَانِيَّةُ وَالْيَهُودِيَّةُ وَالأَمَةُ الْمُسْلِمَةُ يُحْصِنَّ الْحُرَّ الْمُسْلِمَ إِذَا نَكَحَ إِحْدَاهُنَّ فَأَصَابَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1132" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
