<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor); al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab, and he had heard from al-Qasim ibn Muhammad that they said, &quot;When a free man marries a slave-girl and consummates the marriage, she makes him muhsan.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1132]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1132" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1132</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1132 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;All (of the people of knowledge) I have seen said that a slave-girl makes a free man muhsan when he marries her and consummates the marriage.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A slave makes a free woman muhsana when he consummates a marriage with her and a free woman only makes a slave muhsan when he is freed and he is her husband and has had sexual relations with her after he has been set free. If he parts from her before he is free, he is not a muhsan unless he marries her after having been set free and he consummates the marriage.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a slave-girl is married to a free man and then he separates from her before she is set free, his marriage to her does not make her muhsana. She is not muhsana until she has married after she has been set free and she has had intercourse with her husband. That gives her ihsan. If she is the wife of a freeman and then she is set free while she is his wife before he separates from her, the man makes her muhsana if he has intercourse with her after she has been set free.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The christian and jewish free women and the muslim slave-girl all make a muslim free man muhsan when he marries one of them and has intercourse with her</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1132" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1123

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1133)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَسَنِ، ابْنَىْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَلِيِّ بْنِ أَبِي طَالِبٍ عَنْ أَبِيهِمَا، عَنْ عَلِيِّ بْنِ أَبِي طَالِبٍ، رضى الله عنه أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنْ مُتْعَةِ النِّسَاءِ يَوْمَ خَيْبَرَ وَعَنْ أَكْلِ لُحُومِ الْحُمُرِ الإِنْسِيَّةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1133]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1133" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1133</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1133 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Abdullah and Hasan, the sons of Muhammad ibn Ali ibn Abi Talib from their ather, mayAllah be pleased with him, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade temporary marriage with women and the flesh of domestic donkeys on the Day of Khaybar</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1133]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1133" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रात अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मना किया जंग-इ-खैबर के रोज़ मुता&#x27;आ से और गधो के गोश्त खाने से.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1133]</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1133]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mana kiya jang-e-Khaibar ke roz muta&#x27;a se aur gadho ke gosht khane se.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1133]</p>

## Narration 1124

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1134)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1134</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1134 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّ خَوْلَةَ بِنْتَ حَكِيمٍ، دَخَلَتْ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَتْ إِنَّ رَبِيعَةَ بْنَ أُمَيَّةَ اسْتَمْتَعَ بِامْرَأَةٍ فَحَمَلَتْ مِنْهُ ‏.‏ فَخَرَجَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فَزِعًا يَجُرُّ رِدَاءَهُ فَقَالَ هَذِهِ الْمُتْعَةُ وَلَوْ كُنْتُ تَقَدَّمْتُ فِيهَا لَرَجَمْتُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Khawla ibn Hakim (companion); Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr that Khawla ibn Hakim came to Umar ibn al-Khattab and said, &#x27;&#x27;Rabia ibn Umayya made a temporary marriage with a woman and she is pregnant by him.&#x27;&#x27; Umar ibn al-Khattab went out in dismay dragging his cloak, saying, &quot;This temporary marriage, had I come across it, I would have ordered stoning and done away with it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उरवह बिन ज़ुबैर से रिवायत है कि ख़ौला बिन्त-ए-हाकिम हज़रत उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास गयीं और कहा कि राबिया बिन उमय्यह ने एक औरत मौलदाह से मुता&#x27;आ किया था, वह राबिया से हामिला है. पस हज़रत उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) घबरा कर चादर घसीट-ते हुए निकले और कहा यह मुता&#x27;आ है, अगर मैं पहले इसकी मुमानत कर चुका होता तो रज़म करता.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر سے روایت ہے کہ خولہ بنت حکیم حضرت عمر کے پاس گئیں اور کہا کہ ربیعہ بن امیہ نے ایک عورت مولدہ سے متعہ کیا تھا وہ ربیعہ سے حاملہ ہے پس حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ گھبرا کر چادر گھسیٹتے ہوئے نکلے اور کہا یہ متعہ ہے اگر میں پہلے اس کی ممانعت کر چکا ہوتا تو رجم کرتا</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair se riwayat hai ki Khaula bint-e-Hakim Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas gayin aur kaha ki Rabia bin Umayyah ne ek aurat mauladah se muta&#x27;a kiya tha, woh Rabia se hamila hai. Pas Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ghabra kar chadar ghasit-te hue nikle aur kaha yeh muta&#x27;a hai, agar main pehle iski mumanat kar chuka hota toh rajam karta.</p>

## Narration 1125

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1135)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1135]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1135</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1135 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ رَبِيعَةَ بْنَ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، يَقُولُ يَنْكِحُ الْعَبْدُ أَرْبَعَ نِسْوَةٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا أَحْسَنُ مَا سَمِعْتُ فِي ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْعَبْدُ مُخَالِفٌ لِلْمُحَلِّلِ إِنْ أَذِنَ لَهُ سَيِّدُهُ ثَبَتَ نِكَاحُهُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَأْذَنْ لَهُ سَيِّدُهُ فُرِّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا وَالْمُحَلِّلُ يُفَرَّقُ بَيْنَهُمَا عَلَى كُلِّ حَالٍ إِذَا أُرِيدَ بِالنِّكَاحِ التَّحْلِيلُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْعَبْدِ إِذَا مَلَكَتْهُ امْرَأَتُهُ أَوِ الزَّوْجُ يَمْلِكُ امْرَأَتَهُ إِنَّ مِلْكَ كُلِّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمَا صَاحِبَهُ يَكُونُ فَسْخًا بِغَيْرِ طَلاَقٍ وَإِنْ تَرَاجَعَا بِنِكَاحٍ بَعْدُ لَمْ تَكُنْ تِلْكَ الْفُرْقَةُ طَلاَقًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْعَبْدُ إِذَا أَعْتَقَتْهُ امْرَأَتُهُ إِذَا مَلَكَتْهُ وَهِيَ فِي عِدَّةٍ مِنْهُ لَمْ يَتَرَاجَعَا إِلاَّ بِنِكَاحٍ جَدِيدٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Rabia ibn Abd ar- Rahman (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he heard Rabia ibn Abd ar- Rahman say that a slave could marry four women.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1135]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1135</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1135 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;This is the best of what I have heard about the matter.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The slave differs with the muhallil if the slave is given permission by his master for his ex-wife. If his master does not give him permission, he separates them. The muhallil is separated in any case if he intends to make the woman halal by marriage.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a slave is owned by his wife or a husband owns his wife, the possession of each of them is rendered void without divorce. If a man, for instance, is married to a slave-girl, and then he buys her, he must divorce her as a matter of course. They can then re- marry. If they re-marry afterwards, that separation was not divorce.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a slave is freed by his wife who owns him and she is in the idda-period from him, they can only return to each other after she has made another marriage</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिआ बिन अबू अब्दुल रहमान कहते थे घुलम चार औरतों से निकाह कर सकता है. कहा मालिक ने यह क़ौल बोहोत अच्छा है मेरे नज़दीक. कहा मालिक ने घुलम का निकाह मालिक की इजाज़त पर मौक़ूफ़ है वरना तफ़रीक़ की जाये और हलाला का निकाह हर तरह से छोड़ा जाये गा. कहा मालिक ने अगर जॉज ज़ौजा का मालिक हो जाये या ज़ौजा जॉज की मालिक हो जाये तोह निकाह खुद-बा-खुद बग़ैर तलक़ के फ़स्ख़ हो जाये गा खावंद को तीन तलाक़ का इख़्तियार रहे गा. कहा मालिक ने अगर ज़ौजा अपने खावंद को ख़रीद कर आज़ाद कर दे और वह इद्दत में हो तोह वह दोनों नए निक़ाह के बग़ैर नहीं मिल सकते.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابوعبدالرحمن کہتے تھے غلام چار عورتوں سے نکاح کر سکتا ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے یہ قول بہت اچھا ہے میرے نزدیک ۔ کہا مالک نے غلام کا نکاح مالی کی اجازت پر موقوف ہے اگر مالی اجازت دے گا تو صحیح ہوگا ورنہ تفریق کی جائے اور حلالہ کا نکاح ہر طرح سے چھوڑا جائے گا ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر زوج زوجہ کا مالک ہو جائے یا زوجہ زوج کی مالک ہو جائے تو نکاح خوبخود بغیر طلاق کے فسخ ہو جائے گا اب اگر پھر نکاح کریں گے تو خاوند کو تین طلاق کا اختیار رہے گا ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر زوجہ اپنے خاوند کو خرید کر آزاد کر دے اور وہ عدت میں ہو تو وہ دونوں نئے نکاح کے بغیر نہیں مل سکتے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1135]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1135</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1135 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abu Abdul Rahman kehte the ghulam char auraton se nikah kar sakta hai. Kaha Malik ne yeh qaul bohot achha hai mere nazdeek. Kaha Malik ne ghulam ka nikah maalik ki ijazat par mauqoof hai, agar maalik ijazat dega toh sahi hoga warna tafreeq ki jaye aur halala ka nikah har tarah se chhora jaye ga. Kaha Malik ne agar zauj zauja ka maalik ho jaye ya zauja zauj ki maalik ho jaye toh nikah khud-ba-khud baghair talaq ke faskh ho jaye ga, ab agar phir nikah karenge toh khawand ko teen talaq ka ikhtiyar rahe ga. Kaha Malik ne agar zauja apne khawand ko khareed kar azad kar de aur woh iddat mein ho toh woh dono naye nikah ke baghair nahi mil sakte.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1135" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
